    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateInvoicedQtyOnShipmentLine@120(VAR SalesShptLine@1000 : Record 111;QtyToBeInvoiced@1001 : Decimal;QtyToBeInvoicedBase@1002 : Decimal);
    BEGIN
      WITH SalesShptLine DO BEGIN
        "Quantity Invoiced" := "Quantity Invoiced" - QtyToBeInvoiced;
        "Qty. Invoiced (Base)" := "Qty. Invoiced (Base)" - QtyToBeInvoicedBase;
        "Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced" := Quantity - "Quantity Invoiced";
        MODIFY;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ValidateQuantity@1000000004(PItemNo@1000000002 : Code[20];PLocationCode@1000000003 : Code[10];PQuantity@1000000001 : Decimal) RESULT@1000000006 : Boolean;
    VAR
      LItemLedgerEntry@1000000000 : Record 32;
      TQtyItemLedgerEntry@1000000004 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // D34.03
      RESULT := FALSE;
      TQtyItemLedgerEntry := 0;
      LItemLedgerEntry.RESET;
      LItemLedgerEntry.SETCURRENTKEY("Item No.","Location Code","Posting Date");
      IF STRPOS(PItemNo,'&') > 0 THEN
         LItemLedgerEntry.SETFILTER("Item No.",'''+PItemNo+''')
      ELSE
         LItemLedgerEntry.SETFILTER("Item No.",PItemNo);
      LItemLedgerEntry.SETFILTER("Location Code",PLocationCode);
      {LItemLedgerEntry.SETRANGE(Open,TRUE);
      LItemLedgerEntry.SETFILTER(Quantity,'>0');}
      IF LItemLedgerEntry.FIND('-') THEN
      REPEAT
         TQtyItemLedgerEntry := TQtyItemLedgerEntry + LItemLedgerEntry."Remaining Quantity";
      UNTIL LItemLedgerEntry.NEXT = 0;
      RESULT := TQtyItemLedgerEntry >= PQuantity;
      IF (USERID = 'AD') AND (PItemNo = 'ER 012000') THEN
         MESSAGE('TQtyItemLedgerEntry : ' + FORMAT(TQtyItemLedgerEntry));
      // D34.03
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendEmailFP@1000000008(NoSeriesCode@1000000008 : Code[15];TaxSeriesNo@1000000009 : Code[20];StartDate@1000000011 : Date);
    VAR
      SMTP@1000000000 : Codeunit 400;
      StrGetServerName@1000000002 : Text[250];
      SMTPMailSetup@1000000001 : Record 409;
      LSenderName@1000000007 : Text[100];
      LSenderAddress@1000000006 : Text[100];
      LRecipient@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      LSubject@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      LBody@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      NoSeriesLine@1000000010 : Record 309;
      MaxNoSeries@1000000012 : Code[20];
      split_maxseries@1000000013 : Code[20];
      split_taxseries@1000000016 : Code[20];
      int_maxseries@1000000014 : Integer;
      int_taxseries@1000000015 : Integer;
      day@1000000017 : Integer;
      month@1000000018 : Integer;
      year@1000000019 : Integer;
      D@1000000020 : Date;
      NoSeries@1000 : Record 308;
      UserSetup@1001 : Record 91;
    BEGIN
      // D34.03
      day := 31;
      month := 3;
      year := 2013;
      D := DMY2DATE(day, month, year);
      IF StartDate > D THEN BEGIN
          //MESSAGE(FORMAT(D) +'=='+FORMAT(StartDate));

          SMTPMailSetup.FIND('-');
      //    StrGetServerName := CONTEXTURL;

          IF StartDate = 0D THEN
            StartDate := WORKDATE;
          NoSeriesLine.RESET;
          NoSeriesLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Series Code","Starting Date");
          NoSeriesLine.SETRANGE("Series Code",NoSeriesCode);
          NoSeriesLine.SETRANGE("Starting Date",0D,StartDate);
          IF NoSeriesLine.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
            NoSeriesLine.RESET;
            NoSeriesLine.SETRANGE("Starting Date",NoSeriesLine."Starting Date");
            NoSeriesLine.SETRANGE("Series Code",NoSeriesCode);
            NoSeriesLine.SETRANGE(Open,TRUE);
          END;

          IF NoSeriesLine.FINDLAST THEN
             MaxNoSeries := NoSeriesLine."Ending No.";

      //    ERROR(NoSeriesLine.GETFILTERS + '==' + FORMAT(NoSeriesLine.COUNT));

          IF MaxNoSeries <> '' THEN
          BEGIN
           { split_maxseries := CONVERTSTR(MaxNoSeries,'.',',');
            EVALUATE(int_maxseries,SELECTSTR(2,split_maxseries));
            split_taxseries := CONVERTSTR(TaxSeriesNo,'.',',');
            EVALUATE(int_taxseries,SELECTSTR(3,split_taxseries));}
            {
            split_maxseries := CONVERTSTR(MaxNoSeries,'.',',');
            EVALUATE(int_maxseries,SELECTSTR(3,split_maxseries));
            split_taxseries := CONVERTSTR(TaxSeriesNo,'.',',');
            EVALUATE(int_taxseries,SELECTSTR(4,split_taxseries));
            }
            split_maxseries := COPYSTR(MaxNoSeries, 6);
            EVALUATE(int_maxseries, split_maxseries);
            split_taxseries := COPYSTR(TaxSeriesNo, STRLEN(TaxSeriesNo) - STRLEN(split_maxseries) + 1);
            EVALUATE(int_taxseries, split_taxseries);


            //ERROR(NoSeriesCode + '=XX=' + FORMAT(int_maxseries - int_taxseries) );

            //MESSAGE(FORMAT((int_maxseries - int_taxseries)));

            IF(int_maxseries - int_taxseries) = 5000 THEN BEGIN
                //IF (STRPOS(StrGetServerName,'dtsnavision5')=0)AND(SMTPMailSetup."SMTP Server"='Venus') THEN
                //   ERROR('Server Mail ke Live..');

                LSenderName := 'No Reply';
                LSenderAddress := 'app_tracker@datascrip.co.id';
      //          LRecipient := 'benny_aziz@datascrip.co.id;';

                NoSeries.RESET;
                NoSeries.SETFILTER(Code, NoSeriesCode);
                IF NoSeries.FINDFIRST THEN
                  BEGIN
                    UserSetup.RESET;
                    UserSetup.SETFILTER("User ID", NoSeries."User ID (Email Notification)");
                    IF UserSetup.FINDFIRST THEN
                        LRecipient := UserSetup."E-Mail";
                  END;

                LSubject   := '[PERINGATAN]Informasi No. Faktur DTS1 ';
                LBody   := '<html><head><style type="text/css">';
                LBody   := LBody + 'body{font-family: Calibri;font-size: 11pt;}</style></head>';
                LBody   := LBody + '<body><blockquote>Dear Tim Tax, <br><br>No. Faktur DTS1 yang tersisa tinggal 5000<br><br>Regards ';
                LBody   := LBody + '<br>System Admin</blockquote></body></html>';


                SMTP.CreateMessage(LSenderName,LSenderAddress,LRecipient,LSubject,LBody,TRUE);
                SMTP.AddBCC('misitoperation@datascrip.co.id');
                SMTP.Send;
            END;
          END;

            //ERROR(NoSeriesCode + '=XX=' + FORMAT(int_maxseries - int_taxseries) );
      END;
         // ERROR(NoSeriesCode + '=XX=' + FORMAT(int_maxseries - int_taxseries) );
      // D34.03
    END;

    PROCEDURE CopyExternalDocumentLine@1000000001(FromNumber@1000000001 : Code[20];ToNumber@1000000000 : Code[20];WMS@1000000002 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      // D34.03
      //CopyExternalDocumentLine
      ExternalDocumentLine.RESET;

      { DVZ- WMS }
      IF WMS THEN
         ExternalDocumentLine.SETRANGE("Table No.",7320)
      ELSE
         ExternalDocumentLine.SETRANGE("Table No.",36);
      //ExternalDocumentLine.SETFILTER("Table No.",'7320|36');

      ExternalDocumentLine.SETRANGE("No.",FromNumber);
      IF ExternalDocumentLine.FIND('-') THEN
         REPEAT
            ExternalDocumentLine2.INIT;
            ExternalDocumentLine2."Table No." := 110;
            ExternalDocumentLine2."No." := ToNumber;
            ExternalDocumentLine2."Line No." := ExternalDocumentLine."Line No.";
            ExternalDocumentLine2."External Document No." := ExternalDocumentLine."External Document No.";
            ExternalDocumentLine2.INSERT;
         UNTIL ExternalDocumentLine.NEXT = 0;
      ExternalDocumentLine.DELETEALL;
      // D34.03
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalcSlsChrgLineAmt@109(RecSlsHeader@1003 : Record 36);
    VAR
      TempSlsHeader@1000 : TEMPORARY Record 36;
      SalesLine@1001 : Record 37;
      CountSlsLine@1002 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //T26
      CLEAR(TempSlsHeader);
      TempSlsHeader.COPY(RecSlsHeader);

      SalesLine.RESET;
      SalesLine.SETRANGE(SalesLine."Document Type", TempSlsHeader."Document Type");
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine."Document No.", TempSlsHeader."No.");
      //T26 05 Okt 2015 Add Codes
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine."No.",'<>%1','');
      //End T26 05 Okt 2015 Add Codes
      IF SalesLine.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          SalesLine."Sales Charge Line Amount" := TempSlsHeader."Sales Charge Amount" / SalesLine.COUNT;
          SalesLine.MODIFY;

          // BNY 23.02.20
          //IF SalesLine.Quantity * SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" <> 0 THEN  //tutup
          IF SalesLine.Quantity <> 0 THEN
          // soalnya kalo yg WMS "Qty. to Invoice" nya biasanya kosong karena "Qty. to Receive" nya gak otomatis keisi, harus bikin Warehouse Receipt dulu
          BEGIN
            SalesLine."Sales Charge Invcd. Line Amt." := (SalesLine."Sales Charge Line Amount" / SalesLine.Quantity) * SalesLine."Quantity Invoiced";
            SalesLine."Sales Charge Rem. Line Amt." := (SalesLine."Sales Charge Line Amount" / SalesLine.Quantity) * SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice";
            SalesLine.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL SalesLine.NEXT = 0;

      RecSlsHeader := TempSlsHeader;
      //End T26
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSlsChrgInvAmt@110(SlsHeader@1000 : Record 36);
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1002 : Record 36;
      SalesLine@1003 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      //T26
      SalesHeader.RESET;
      SalesHeader.GET(SlsHeader."Document Type", SlsHeader."No.");

      SalesLine.RESET;
      SalesLine.SETRANGE(SalesLine."Document Type", SalesHeader."Document Type");
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine."Document No.", SalesHeader."No.");
      IF SalesLine.FIND('-') THEN
      BEGIN
        SalesLine.CALCSUMS(SalesLine."Sales Charge Invcd. Line Amt.");
        SalesHeader."Sales Charge Amt. Invcd." := SalesLine."Sales Charge Invcd. Line Amt.";
        SalesHeader.MODIFY;
      END;
      //End T26
    END;

    PROCEDURE SlsChrgLineAmtUpdToSlsChrgAmtHdr@111(RecSlsLine@1000 : Record 37;RecSlsChrgLineAmt@1004 : Decimal;xRecSlsChrgLineAmt@1005 : Decimal);
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1001 : Record 36;
      SlsChrgLineAmt@1002 : Decimal;
      xSlsChrgLineAmt@1003 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      //T26
      SalesHeader.RESET;
      SalesHeader.GET(RecSlsLine."Document Type", RecSlsLine."Document No.");
      SalesHeader."Sales Charge Amount" := SalesHeader."Sales Charge Amount" + (RecSlsChrgLineAmt - xRecSlsChrgLineAmt);
      SalesHeader.MODIFY;
      //End T26
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalcSlsChrgLineAmt2@113(RecSlsHeader@1003 : Record 36);
    VAR
      TempSlsHeader@1000 : TEMPORARY Record 36;
      SalesLine@1001 : Record 37;
      CountSlsLine@1002 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //T26
      SalesLine.RESET;
      SalesLine.SETRANGE(SalesLine."Document Type", RecSlsHeader."Document Type");
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine."Document No.", RecSlsHeader."No.");
      //T26 05 Okt 2015 Add Codes
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine."No.",'<>%1','');
      //End T26 05 Okt 2015 Add Codes
      IF SalesLine.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          IF SalesLine.Quantity * SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" <> 0 THEN
          BEGIN
            SalesLine."Sales Charge Invcd. Line Amt." := (SalesLine."Sales Charge Line Amount" / SalesLine.Quantity) * SalesLine."Quantity Invoiced";
            SalesLine."Sales Charge Rem. Line Amt." := (SalesLine."Sales Charge Line Amount" / SalesLine.Quantity) * SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice";
            SalesLine.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL SalesLine.NEXT = 0;
      //End T26
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSlsChrgRemLineAmt@112(RecSlsHeader@1000 : Record 36);
    VAR
      SlsLine@1001 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      //T26
      SlsLine.RESET;
      SlsLine.SETRANGE(SlsLine."Document Type", RecSlsHeader."Document Type");
      SlsLine.SETFILTER(SlsLine."Document No.", RecSlsHeader."No.");
      SlsLine.SETFILTER(SlsLine."Quantity Invoiced", '<>%1', 0);
      IF SlsLine.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          SlsLine."Sales Charge Rem. Line Amt." := (SlsLine."Sales Charge Line Amount" / SlsLine.Quantity) * SlsLine."Qty. to Invoice";
          SlsLine."Sales Charge Invcd. Line Amt." := (SlsLine."Sales Charge Line Amount" / SlsLine.Quantity) * SlsLine."Quantity Invoiced";
          SlsLine.MODIFY;
        UNTIL SlsLine.NEXT = 0;
      //End T26
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalcInsentifTambahanLineAmt@116(RecSlsHeader@1003 : Record 36);
    VAR
      TempSlsHeader@1000 : TEMPORARY Record 36;
      SalesLine@1001 : Record 37;
      CountSlsLine@1002 : Integer;
      InsentifTambahanInvAmt@1004 : Decimal;
      SlsInvHeader@1005 : Record 112;
      SlsInvLine@1006 : Record 113;
    BEGIN
      //T25
      CLEAR(TempSlsHeader);
      TempSlsHeader.COPY(RecSlsHeader);

      SalesLine.RESET;
      SalesLine.SETRANGE(SalesLine."Document Type", TempSlsHeader."Document Type");
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine."Document No.", TempSlsHeader."No.");
      //T25.3
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine.Type, '<>%1', SalesLine.Type::" ");
      //End T25.3
      //T25.10
      //SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine."Quantity Invoiced", '%1', 0);  //MPT Closed - 23/01/17 agar bisa dimasukan berulang2 nilai invoice tambahanya.
      //End T25.10
      IF SalesLine.FIND('-') THEN
        REPEAT
          SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Line Amount" := TempSlsHeader."Insentif Tambahan Amount" / SalesLine.COUNT;
          //SalesLine.MODIFY;

          IF SalesLine.Quantity <> 0 THEN
          BEGIN
            //T25.10
            {
            InsentifTambahanInvAmt := 0;

            SlsInvHeader.RESET;
            SlsInvHeader.SETFILTER(SlsInvHeader."Order No.", TempSlsHeader."No.");
            IF SlsInvHeader.FIND('-')THEN
              REPEAT
                SlsInvLine.RESET;
                SlsInvLine.SETFILTER(SlsInvLine."No.", SlsInvHeader."No.");
                SlsInvLine.SETRANGE(SlsInvLine.Type, SalesLine.Type);
                SlsInvLine.SETFILTER(SlsInvLine."No.", SalesLine."No.");
                SlsInvLine.SETRANGE(SlsInvLine."Line No.", SalesLine."Line No.");
                SlsInvLine.SETFILTER(SlsInvLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt", '<>%1', 0);
                IF SlsInvLine.FINDFIRST THEN
                  InsentifTambahanInvAmt += SalesInvLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt";
              UNTIL SlsInvHeader.NEXT = 0;
            }

            //SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt" := (SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Line Amount" / SalesLine.Quantity) * SalesLine."Quantity Invoiced";
            //SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt" := InsentifTambahanInvAmt;   //MPT Closed - 23/01/17 karena perhitungan dilakukan pada saat posting invoice.
            //End T25.10
            //SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Rem.Line Amt" := SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Line Amount" - SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt";  //MPT closed 23/01/2017 : base on tiket id servicedesck : 8845
            SalesLine.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL SalesLine.NEXT = 0;

      RecSlsHeader := TempSlsHeader;
      //End T25
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsentifTmbhnLineAmtUpdToInsentifTmbhnAmtHdr@114(RecSlsLine@1000 : Record 37;RecInsentifTmbhnLineAmt@1004 : Decimal;xRecInsentifTmbhnLineAmt@1005 : Decimal);
    VAR

    PROCEDURE CalcInsentifTambahanLineAmt2@115(RecSlsHeader@1003 : Record 36;tipe@1004 : Integer);
    VAR
      TempSlsHeader@1000 : TEMPORARY Record 36;
      SalesLine@1001 : Record 37;
      CountSlsLine@1002 : Integer;
      SalesHeader@1005 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      //T25
      SalesLine.RESET;
      SalesLine.SETRANGE(SalesLine."Document Type", RecSlsHeader."Document Type");
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine."Document No.", RecSlsHeader."No.");
      //T25.3
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine.Type, '<>%1', SalesLine.Type::" ");
      //End T25.3
      IF SalesLine.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          IF SalesLine.Quantity <> 0 THEN
          BEGIN
            //SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt" := (SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Line Amount" / SalesLine.Quantity) * SalesLine."Quantity Invoiced"; //MPT closed 18/01/2017 : base on tiket id servicedesck : 8845
            //SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Rem.Line Amt" := SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Line Amount" - SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt"; //MPT closed 23/01/2017 : base on tiket id servicedesck : 8845
            IF tipe = 2 THEN BEGIN   //MPT - tipe 2 digunakan untuk posting invoic
              SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt" := SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Line Amount" + SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt";
              SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Line Amount" := 0;
            END;
            SalesLine.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL SalesLine.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      //End T25

      //MPT - 23/01/2016 untuk mengosongkan nilai "Insentif Tambahan Amount" pada saat posting invoice & tipe 2 digunakan untuk posting invoice.
      IF tipe = 2 THEN BEGIN
        SalesHeader.GET(RecSlsHeader."Document Type", RecSlsHeader."No.");
        SalesHeader."Insentif Tambahan Amount" := 0;
        SalesHeader.MODIFY;
      END;
      //MPT - 23/01/2016 untuk mengosongkan nilai "Insentif Tambahan Amount" pada saat posting invoice & tipe 2 digunakan untuk posting invoice.
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateInsentifTambahanHdr@117(SlsHeader@1000 : Record 36);
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1002 : Record 36;
      SalesLine@1003 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      //T25
      SalesHeader.RESET;
      SalesHeader.GET(SlsHeader."Document Type", SlsHeader."No.");

      SalesLine.RESET;
      SalesLine.SETRANGE(SalesLine."Document Type", SalesHeader."Document Type");
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine."Document No.", SalesHeader."No.");
      //T25.3
      SalesLine.SETFILTER(SalesLine.Type, '<>%1', SalesLine.Type::" ");
      //End T25.3
      IF SalesLine.FIND('-') THEN
      BEGIN
        SalesLine.CALCSUMS(SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Rem.Line Amt", SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt");
        SalesHeader."Insentif Tambahan Rem. Amt." := SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Rem.Line Amt";
        SalesHeader."Insentif Tambahan Invd. Amt." := SalesLine."Insentif Tambahan Inv.Line Amt";
        SalesHeader.MODIFY;
      END;
      //End T25
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE PostInsentifTambahanEntry@118(SalesHeader2@1000 : Record 36;InsentifTambahanInvAmt2@1005 : Decimal;DocType@1002 : Option;DocNo@1003 : Code[20];ExtDocNo@1004 : Code[35];SourceCode@1007 : Code[10];GLAccount@1006 : Code[20];DimSetId@1008 : Integer);
    VAR
      GenJnlLine2@1001 : Record 81;
    BEGIN

      WITH SalesHeader2 DO BEGIN
        GenJnlLine2.INIT;

        GenJnlLine2."Document Date" := "Document Date";
        GenJnlLine2.Description := "Posting Description";
        GenJnlLine2."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := "Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";

        GenJnlLine2."Document Type" := DocType;
        GenJnlLine2."Document No." := DocNo;
        GenJnlLine2."External Document No." := ExtDocNo;
        GenJnlLine2."Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
        GenJnlLine2.Amount := InsentifTambahanInvAmt2;
        GenJnlLine2."Source Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
        GenJnlLine2."Source Currency Amount" := InsentifTambahanInvAmt2;
        GenJnlLine2."Amount (LCY)" := InsentifTambahanInvAmt2;
        IF "Currency Code" = '' THEN
          GenJnlLine2."Currency Factor" := 1
        ELSE
          GenJnlLine2."Currency Factor" := "Currency Factor";
        GenJnlLine2.Correction := Correction;
        GenJnlLine2."Sales/Purch. (LCY)" := InsentifTambahanInvAmt2;
        GenJnlLine2."Sell-to/Buy-from No." := "Sell-to Customer No.";
        GenJnlLine2."Bill-to/Pay-to No." := "Bill-to Customer No.";
        GenJnlLine2."Salespers./Purch. Code" := "Salesperson Code";
        GenJnlLine2."System-Created Entry" := TRUE;
        GenJnlLine2."On Hold" := "On Hold";
        //T25.9
        IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::Order THEN
        BEGIN
          GenJnlLine2."Applies-to Doc. Type" := "Applies-to Doc. Type";
          GenJnlLine2."Applies-to Doc. No." := "Applies-to Doc. No.";
          GenJnlLine2."Applies-to ID" := "Applies-to ID";
        END
        ELSE
        BEGIN
          GenJnlLine2."Applies-to Doc. Type" := "Applies-to Doc. Type"::" ";
          GenJnlLine2."Applies-to Doc. No." := '';
          GenJnlLine2."Applies-to ID" := '';
        END;
        //End T25.9
        GenJnlLine2."Allow Application" := "Bal. Account No." = '';
        GenJnlLine2."Due Date" := "Due Date";
        GenJnlLine2."Direct Debit Mandate ID" := "Direct Debit Mandate ID";
        GenJnlLine2."Payment Terms Code" := "Payment Terms Code";
        GenJnlLine2."Payment Method Code" := "Payment Method Code";
        GenJnlLine2."Source Type" := GenJnlLine2."Source Type"::Customer;
        GenJnlLine2."Source No." := "Bill-to Customer No.";
        GenJnlLine2."Source Code" := SourceCode;
        GenJnlLine2."Posting No. Series" := "Posting No. Series";
        GenJnlLine2."IC Partner Code" := "Sell-to IC Partner Code";
        GenJnlPostLine.RunWithCheck(GenJnlLine2);
      END;
      //End T25
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsUpdSalesItemCustRegister@1000000003(PTransaction@1000000004 : 'Invoice,Credit Memo,Nota Kredit';PType@1000000002 : 'Item,Resource';PItemNo@1000000000 : Code[20];PCustNo@1000000003 : Code[20];PPostingDate@1000000001 : Date;PQuantity@1000000012 : Decimal;PLineAmount@1000000005 : Decimal;PInvDiscAmount@1000000006 : Decimal;PLineDiscAmount@1000000007 : Decimal;PExtraDiscAmount@1000000008 : Decimal;PLCY@1000000014 : Boolean);
    VAR
      LSalesItem@1000000010 : Record 50016;
      LItem@1000000009 : Record 27;
      LResource@1000000013 : Record 156;
      LModeInsert@1000000011 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      IF PItemNo = '' THEN
         EXIT;
      IF LSalesItem.GET(PType,PItemNo,PCustNo,PPostingDate) THEN
         LModeInsert := FALSE
      ELSE
         LModeInsert := TRUE;

      IF LModeInsert THEN
         LSalesItem.INIT;

      LSalesItem.Type := PType;
      LSalesItem."Item/Resource No." := PItemNo;
      LSalesItem."Customer No." := PCustNo;
      LSalesItem."Posting Date" := PPostingDate;
      IF LItem.GET(PItemNo) THEN
      BEGIN
         LSalesItem."Item Category Code" := LItem."Item Category Code";
         LSalesItem."Product Group Code" := LItem."Product Group Code";
      END
      ELSE
      IF LResource.GET(PItemNo) THEN
      BEGIN
         LSalesItem."Item Category Code" := LResource."Item Category Code";
         LSalesItem."Product Group Code" := LResource."Product Group Code";
      END;

      CASE PTransaction OF
         PTransaction::Invoice :
         BEGIN
            LSalesItem."Inv. Quantity" := LSalesItem."Inv. Quantity" + PQuantity;
            IF PLCY THEN
            BEGIN
               LSalesItem."Inv. Line Amount (LCY)" := LSalesItem."Inv. Line Amount (LCY)" + PLineAmount;
               LSalesItem."Inv. Disc. Amount (LCY)" := LSalesItem."Inv. Disc. Amount (LCY)" + PInvDiscAmount;
               LSalesItem."Inv. Line Disc. Amount (LCY)" := LSalesItem."Inv. Line Disc. Amount (LCY)" + PLineDiscAmount;
               LSalesItem."Inv. Extra Disc. Amount (LCY)" := LSalesItem."Inv. Extra Disc. Amount (LCY)" + PExtraDiscAmount;
            END
            ELSE
            BEGIN
               LSalesItem."Inv. Line Amount (ACY)" := LSalesItem."Inv. Line Amount (ACY)" + PLineAmount;
               LSalesItem."Inv. Disc. Amount (ACY)" := LSalesItem."Inv. Disc. Amount (ACY)" + PInvDiscAmount;
               LSalesItem."Inv. Line Disc. Amount (ACY)" := LSalesItem."Inv. Line Disc. Amount (ACY)" + PLineDiscAmount;
               LSalesItem."Inv. Extra Disc. Amount (ACY)" := LSalesItem."Inv. Extra Disc. Amount (ACY)" + PExtraDiscAmount;
            END
         END;
         PTransaction::"Credit Memo" :
         BEGIN
            LSalesItem."Cm. Quantity" := LSalesItem."Cm. Quantity" + PQuantity;
            IF PLCY THEN
            BEGIN
               LSalesItem."Cm. Line Amount (LCY)" := LSalesItem."Cm. Line Amount (LCY)" + PLineAmount;
               LSalesItem."Cm. Disc. Amount (LCY)" := LSalesItem."Cm. Disc. Amount (LCY)" + PInvDiscAmount;
               LSalesItem."Cm. Line Disc. Amount (LCY)" := LSalesItem."Cm. Line Disc. Amount (LCY)" + PLineDiscAmount;
               LSalesItem."Cm. Extra Disc. Amount (LCY)" := LSalesItem."Cm. Extra Disc. Amount (LCY)" + PExtraDiscAmount;
            END
            ELSE
            BEGIN
               LSalesItem."Cm. Line Amount (ACY)" := LSalesItem."Cm. Line Amount (ACY)" + PLineAmount;
               LSalesItem."Cm. Disc. Amount (ACY)" := LSalesItem."Cm. Disc. Amount (ACY)" + PInvDiscAmount;
               LSalesItem."Cm. Line Disc. Amount (ACY)" := LSalesItem."Cm. Line Disc. Amount (ACY)" + PLineDiscAmount;
               LSalesItem."Cm. Extra Disc. Amount (ACY)" := LSalesItem."Cm. Extra Disc. Amount (ACY)" + PExtraDiscAmount;
            END
         END;
         PTransaction::"Nota Kredit" :
         BEGIN
            LSalesItem."NK Quantity" := LSalesItem."NK Quantity" + PQuantity;
            IF PLCY THEN
               LSalesItem."NK Sales Amount Actual (LCY)" := LSalesItem."NK Sales Amount Actual (LCY)" + PLineAmount
            ELSE
               LSalesItem."NK Sales Amount Actual (LCY)" := LSalesItem."NK Sales Amount Actual (ACY)" + PLineAmount;
         END;
      END;

      IF LModeInsert THEN
         LSalesItem.INSERT
      ELSE
         LSalesItem.MODIFY;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSalesInvoiceCreditMemo@1000000002(PDocNo@1000000002 : Code[20];PLineNo@1000000027 : Integer;PType@1000000000 : 'Invoice,Credit Memo');
    VAR
      LSalesInvHeader@1000000026 : Record 112;
      LSalesInvLine@1000000025 : Record 113;
      LSalesCrMemoHeader@1000000024 : Record 114;
      LSalesCrMemoLine@1000000023 : Record 115;
      LValEntry@1000000022 : Record 5802;
      LType@1000000001 : Integer;
      LCurrRate@1000000021 : Decimal;
      LQtyInv@1000000020 : Decimal;
      LGrossInv@1000000019 : Decimal;
      LLineDiscInv@1000000018 : Decimal;
      LNettoInv@1000000017 : Decimal;
      LExtraDiscInv@1000000016 : Decimal;
      LNett2Inv@1000000015 : Decimal;
      LQtyCrMemo@1000000014 : Decimal;
      LGrossCrMemo@1000000013 : Decimal;
      LLineDiscCrMemo@1000000012 : Decimal;
      LNettoCrMemo@1000000011 : Decimal;
      LExtraDiscCrMemo@1000000010 : Decimal;
      LNett2CrMemo@1000000009 : Decimal;
      LQtyNK@1000000008 : Decimal;
      LGrossNK@1000000007 : Decimal;
      LLineDiscNK@1000000006 : Decimal;
      LNettoNK@1000000005 : Decimal;
      LExtraDiscNK@1000000004 : Decimal;
      LNett2NK@1000000003 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      CASE PType OF
      PType::Invoice :
         BEGIN
            IF LSalesInvHeader.GET(PDocNo) THEN
            BEGIN
               LCurrRate := 1;
               IF LSalesInvHeader."Currency Code" <> '' THEN
                  IF LSalesInvHeader."Currency Factor" <> 0 THEN
                     LCurrRate := 1 / LSalesInvHeader."Currency Factor";
               IF LSalesInvHeader."VAT Bus. Posting Group" = 'TD' THEN
               BEGIN
                  LType := 0;
                  LValEntry.RESET;
                  LValEntry.SETCURRENTKEY("Document No.");
                  LValEntry.SETFILTER("Document No.",LSalesInvHeader."No.");
                  // Kunci untuk mendapatkan relasi antara invoice line dengan value entry adalah document line no.
                  LValEntry.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",PLineNo);
                  IF LValEntry.FIND('-') THEN
                  REPEAT
                     InsUpdSalesItemCustRegister(2,LType,LValEntry."Item No.",LSalesInvHeader."Sell-to Customer No.",
                     LSalesInvHeader."Posting Date",1,
                     LValEntry."Sales Amount (Actual)"*-1,0,0,0,TRUE);
                  UNTIL LValEntry.NEXT = 0;
               END
               ELSE
               BEGIN
                  LSalesInvLine.RESET;
                  LSalesInvLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Document No.",Type,"No.");
                  LSalesInvLine.SETFILTER("Document No.",LSalesInvHeader."No.");
                  LSalesInvLine.SETRANGE("Line No.",PLineNo);
                  IF LSalesInvLine.FIND('-') THEN
                  REPEAT
                     CASE LSalesInvLine.Type OF
                        LSalesInvLine.Type::Item : LType := 0;
                        LSalesInvLine.Type::Resource : LType := 1;
                     END;
                     IF LCurrRate = 1 THEN
                     BEGIN
                        InsUpdSalesItemCustRegister(0,LType,LSalesInvLine."No.",LSalesInvHeader."Sell-to Customer No.",
                        LSalesInvHeader."Posting Date",LSalesInvLine.Quantity,
                        LSalesInvLine."Line Amount",
                        LSalesInvLine."Inv. Discount Amount",
                        LSalesInvLine."Line Discount Amount",
                        LSalesInvLine."Extra Disc",TRUE);
                     END
                     ELSE
                     BEGIN
                        InsUpdSalesItemCustRegister(0,LType,LSalesInvLine."No.",LSalesInvHeader."Sell-to Customer No.",
                        LSalesInvHeader."Posting Date",LSalesInvLine.Quantity,
                        LSalesInvLine."Line Amount"*LCurrRate,
                        LSalesInvLine."Inv. Discount Amount"*LCurrRate,
                        LSalesInvLine."Line Discount Amount"*LCurrRate,
                        LSalesInvLine."Extra Disc"*LCurrRate,TRUE);
                     END;
                  UNTIL LSalesInvLine.NEXT = 0;
               END;
            END;
         END;
      PType::"Credit Memo" :
         BEGIN
            IF LSalesCrMemoHeader.GET(PDocNo) THEN
            BEGIN
               LCurrRate := 1;
               IF LSalesCrMemoHeader."Currency Code" <> '' THEN
                  IF LSalesCrMemoHeader."Currency Factor" <> 0 THEN
                     LCurrRate := 1 / LSalesCrMemoHeader."Currency Factor";
               IF LSalesCrMemoHeader."Pre-Assigned No." = '' THEN
               BEGIN
                  LSalesCrMemoLine.RESET;
                  LSalesCrMemoLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Document No.",Type,"No.");
                  LSalesCrMemoLine.SETFILTER("Document No.",LSalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
                  LSalesCrMemoLine.SETRANGE("Line No.",PLineNo);
                  IF LSalesCrMemoLine.FIND('-') THEN
                  REPEAT
                     CASE LSalesCrMemoLine.Type OF
                        LSalesCrMemoLine.Type::Item : LType := 0;
                        LSalesCrMemoLine.Type::Resource : LType := 1;
                     END;
                     IF LCurrRate = 1 THEN
                     BEGIN
                        InsUpdSalesItemCustRegister(1,LType,LSalesCrMemoLine."No.",LSalesCrMemoHeader."Sell-to Customer No.",
                        LSalesCrMemoHeader."Posting Date",LSalesCrMemoLine.Quantity,
                        LSalesCrMemoLine."Line Amount",
                        LSalesCrMemoLine."Inv. Discount Amount",
                        LSalesCrMemoLine."Line Discount Amount",
                        LSalesCrMemoLine."Extra Disc",TRUE);
                     END
                     ELSE
                     BEGIN
                        InsUpdSalesItemCustRegister(1,LType,LSalesCrMemoLine."No.",LSalesCrMemoHeader."Sell-to Customer No.",
                        LSalesCrMemoHeader."Posting Date",LSalesCrMemoLine.Quantity,
                        LSalesCrMemoLine."Line Amount"*LCurrRate,
                        LSalesCrMemoLine."Inv. Discount Amount"*LCurrRate,
                        LSalesCrMemoLine."Line Discount Amount"*LCurrRate,
                        LSalesCrMemoLine."Extra Disc"*LCurrRate,TRUE);
                     END;
                  UNTIL LSalesCrMemoLine.NEXT = 0;
               END
               ELSE
               BEGIN
                  LType := 0;
                  LValEntry.RESET;
                  LValEntry.SETCURRENTKEY("Document No.");
                  LValEntry.SETFILTER("Document No.",PDocNo);
                  // Kunci untuk mendapatkan relasi antara credit memo line dengan value entry adalah document line no.
                  LValEntry.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",PLineNo);
                  IF LValEntry.FIND('-') THEN
                  REPEAT
                     InsUpdSalesItemCustRegister(2,LType,LValEntry."Item No.",LSalesCrMemoHeader."Sell-to Customer No.",
                     LSalesCrMemoHeader."Posting Date",1,
                     LValEntry."Sales Amount (Actual)"*-1,0,0,0,TRUE);
                  UNTIL LValEntry.NEXT = 0;
               END;
            END;
         END;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckDeptMAS@121(sls_header@1003 : Record 36);
    VAR
      recSalesHeader@1000 : Record 36;
      DateAkhir@1001 : Date;
      DateLama@1002 : Date;
    BEGIN
      IF sls_header."Document Type" = sls_header."Document Type"::Order THEN
      BEGIN
          IF (sls_header."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" = '') THEN
             ERROR('Dept. Code masih kosong');

          IF NOT (sls_header."No." IN ['371-16-01582']) THEN
          BEGIN
              DateAkhir := 010117D;
              DateLama := 12319999D;
              recSalesHeader.RESET;
              recSalesHeader.SETFILTER("Responsibility Center",'MAS JKT|KPC DAERAH|HYPER');
              recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Posting Date",0D,DateAkhir);
              recSalesHeader.SETFILTER("Shortcut Dimension 1 Code",'%1..%2','401','499');
              recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("No.",sls_header."No.");

              IF recSalesHeader.COUNT > 0 THEN
                 ERROR('Dept Code kepala 4 baru digunakan mulai 2 Jan 2017');

              recSalesHeader.RESET;
              recSalesHeader.SETFILTER("Responsibility Center",'MAS JKT|KPC DAERAH|HYPER|E-COMM');
              recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("Posting Date",DateAkhir,DateLama);
              recSalesHeader.SETFILTER("Shortcut Dimension 1 Code",'%1..%2','301','399');
              recSalesHeader.SETRANGE("No.",sls_header."No.");

              IF recSalesHeader.COUNT > 0 THEN
                 ERROR('Dept Code kepala 3 sudah tidak boleh digunakan mulai 2 Jan 2017');
          END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetInvBayangan@122(VATBusPostGroup@1000 : Code[30]) : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      EXIT (VATBusPostGroup IN ['BATAM']);
    END;
